array of references\n            return ''.join(chr(int(f[v][()].flat[0])) for v in val.flat)\n        if val.dtype.kind in ('U', 'S'):\n            return str(val.flat[0])\n        # numeric array that represents characters (uint16)\n        if val.dtype.kind in ('u', 'i') and val.size < 200:\n            try:\n                return ''.join(chr(int(c)) for c in val.flat)\n            except (ValueError, OverflowError):\n                pass\n    if isinstance(val, (np.integer, np.floating)):\n        return str(val)\n    return str(val)\n\n\ndef h5_read_array(f, ref_or_dataset):\n    \"\"\"Read a numeric array from HDF5, handling object references.\"\"\"\n    if isinstance(ref_or_dataset, h5py.h5r.Reference):\n        ds = f[ref_or_dataset]\n    else:\n        ds = ref_or_dataset\n    val = ds[()]\n    if isinstance(val, np.ndarray):\n        return val.astype(np.float64)\n    return np.array([float(val)])\n\n\ndef h5_read_cell_array_of_arrays(f, dataset):\n    \"\"\"Read a MATLAB cell array of numeric arrays from HDF5.\"\"\"\n    refs = dataset[()]\n    result = []\n    for ref in refs.flat:\n        arr = h5_read_array(f, ref)\n        result.append(arr)\n    return result\n\n\n# =============================================================================\n# Signal processing functions (matching MATLAB reference code)\n# =============================================================================\n\ndef causal_gaussian_smooth(x, N, bctype='reflect'):\n    \"\"\"\n    Replicate mySmooth.m: causal Gaussian smoothing.\n    x: (time,) or (time, features) array\n    N: window size\n    bctype: 'reflect', 'zeropad', or 'none'\n    \"\"\"\n    if N <= 1:\n        return x\n\n    was_1d = (x.ndim == 1)\n    if was_1d:\n        x = x[:, np.newaxis]\n\n    # Build kernel\n    kern = np.array(sig_windows.gaussian(N, std=(N - 1) / (2 * 2.5)))\n    # Make causal: zero out first half\n    kern[:N // 2] = 0\n    kern = kern / kern.sum()\n\n    # Handle boundary conditions\n    if bctype == 'reflect':\n        x_filt = np.concatenate([x[:N, :], x], axis=0)\n        trim = N\n    elif bctype == 'zeropad':\n        x_filt = np.concatenate([np.zeros((N, x.shape[1])), x], axis=0)\n        trim = N\n    else:\n        x_filt = x\n        trim = 0\n\n    # Convolve each column\n    out = np.zeros_like(x_filt)\n    for j in range(x_filt.shape[1]):\n        out[:, j] = np.convolve(x_filt[:, j], kern, mode='same')\n    out = out[trim:, :]\n\n    if was_1d:\n        return out[:, 0]\n    return out\n\n\n# =============================================================================\n# Data loading functions\n# =============================================================================\n\ndef _detect_file_format(data_fn):\n    \"\"\"Detect if a .mat file is HDF5 (v7.3) or v5 format.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        f = h5py.File(data_fn, 'r')\n        f.close()\n        return 'hdf5'\n    except Exception:\n        return 'v5'\n\n\ndef _load_raw_data_v5(data_fn, probes):\n    \"\"\"Load raw data from MATLAB v5 format file. Returns common dict format.\"\"\"\n    import scipy.io as sio\n    mat = sio.loadmat(data_fn, squeeze_me=False)\n    obj = mat['obj']\n\n    # --- bp ---\n    bp_raw = obj['bp'][0, 0]\n    bp = {}\n    bp['Ntrials'] = int(bp_raw['Ntrials'][0, 0].flat[0])\n    ntrials = bp['Ntrials']\n\n    for field in ['hit', 'miss', 'no', 'early', 'L', 'R', 'autowater']:\n        bp[field] = bp_raw[field][0, 0].flatten().astype(np.float64)[:ntrials]\n\n    stim_enable = np.zeros(ntrials)\n    if 'stim' in bp_raw.dtype.names:\n        stim_raw = bp_raw['stim'][0, 0]\n        if 'enable' in stim_raw.dtype.names:\n            stim_enable = stim_raw['enable'][0, 0].flatten().astype(np.float64)[:ntrials]\n    bp['stim_enable'] = stim_enable\n\n    ev = {}\n    ev_raw = bp_raw['ev'][0, 0]\n    for field in ['goCue', 'sample', 'delay']:\n        ev[field] = ev_raw[field][0, 0].flatten().astype(np.float64)[:ntrials]\n    bp['ev'] = ev\n\n    # --- vidshift ---\n    vidshift = 0.5\n    try:\n        bit_start_arr = ev_raw['bitStart'][0, 0].flatten().astype(np.float64)\n        bit_start = np.nanmedian(bit_start_arr)\n        sglx_raw = obj['sglx'][0, 0]\n        fs = sglx_raw['fs'][0, 0].flat[0]\n        bitcode_raw = sglx_raw['bitcode'][0, 0]\n        bitcode_bitstart = np.nanmedian(bitcode_raw['bitstart'][0, 0].flatten().astype(np.float64))\n        vid_file_offset = bitcode_bitstart / fs\n        vidshift = vid_file_offset - bit_start\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n\n    # --- clu ---\n    clu_raw = obj['clu'][0, 0]\n    all_spike_times = []\n    all_spike_trials = []\n    all_qualities = []\n\n    for probe_num in probes:\n        probe_idx = probe_num - 1\n        probe_data = clu_raw[0, probe_idx]\n        n_units = probe_data.shape[1] if probe_data.ndim > 1 else probe_data.shape[0]\n        for i in range(n_units):\n            unit = probe_data[0, i] if probe_data.ndim > 1 else probe_data[i]\n            quality = str(unit['quality'].flat[0]).strip().lower() if unit['quality'].size > 0 else ''\n            trialtm = unit['trialtm'].flatten().astype(np.float64)\n            trial = unit['trial'].flatten().astype(np.float64)\n            all_qualities.append(quality)\n            all_spike_times.append(trialtm)\n            all_spike_trials.append(trial)\n\n    # --- traj (for velocity computation) ---\n    traj_data = None\n    try:\n        traj_raw = obj['traj'][0, 0]\n        traj_data = {'views': []}\n        for view_idx in range(traj_raw.shape[1]):\n            view_data = traj_raw[0, view_idx]\n            view_trials = []\n            feat_names = None\n            for t in range(min(ntrials, view_data.shape[1] if view_data.ndim > 1 else view_data.shape[0])):\n                trial_data = view_data[0, t] if view_data.ndim > 1 else view_data[t]\n                ts = trial_data['ts'].astype(np.float64)  # (nFrames, 3, nFeats) in v5\n                ft = trial_data['frameTimes'].flatten().astype(np.float64)\n                if feat_names is None:\n                    fn_raw = trial_data['featNames'].flatten()\n                    feat_names = [str(x.flat[0]).strip() for x in fn_raw]\n                view_trials.append({'ts': ts, 'frameTimes': ft, 'ts_format': 'v5'})\n            traj_data['views'].append({'trials': view_trials, 'feat_names': feat_names})\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n\n    return {\n        'bp': bp, 'vidshift': vidshift, 'ntrials': ntrials,\n        'all_spike_times': all_spike_times, 'all_spike_trials': all_spike_trials,\n        'all_qualities': all_qualities, 'traj_data': traj_data,\n        'n_units_raw': len(all_qualities),\n    }\n\n\ndef _load_raw_data_hdf5(data_fn, probes):\n    \"\"\"Load raw data from HDF5 (v7.3) format file. Returns common dict format.\"\"\"\n    f = h5py.File(data_fn, 'r')\n    obj = f['obj']\n\n    bp = {}\n    bp_group = obj['bp']\n    bp['Ntrials'] = int(bp_group['Ntrials'][()].flat[0])\n    ntrials = bp['Ntrials']\n\n    for field in ['hit', 'miss', 'no', 'early', 'L', 'R', 'autowater']:\n        bp[field] = bp_group[field][()].flatten().astype(np.float64)[:ntrials]\n\n    stim_enable = np.zeros(ntrials)\n    if 'stim' in bp_group:\n        stim_grp = bp_group['stim']\n        if 'enable' in stim_grp:\n            stim_enable = stim_grp['enable'][()].flatten().astype(np.float64)[:ntrials]\n    bp['stim_enable'] = stim_enable\n\n    ev = {}\n    ev_group = bp_group['ev']\n    for field in ['goCue', 'sample', 'delay']:\n        ev[field] = ev_group[field][()].flatten().astype(np.float64)[:ntrials]\n    bp['ev'] = ev\n\n    vidshift = 0.5\n    try:\n        bit_start = np.nanmedian(bp_group['ev']['bitStart'][()].flatten())\n        sglx = obj['sglx']\n        fs = sglx['fs'][()].flat[0]\n        bitcode_bitstart = np.nanmedian(sglx['bitcode']['bitstart'][()].flatten())\n        vid_file_offset = bitcode_bitstart / fs\n        vidshift = vid_file_offset - bit_start\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n\n    clu_refs = obj['clu'][()].flatten()\n    all_spike_times = []\n    all_spike_trials = []\n    all_qualities = []\n\n    for probe_num in probes:\n        probe_idx = probe_num - 1\n        probe_ref = clu_refs[probe_idx]\n        probe_group = f[probe_ref]\n        quality_refs = probe_group['quality'][()].flatten()\n        trialtm_refs = probe_group['trialtm'][()].flatten()\n        trial_refs = probe_group['trial'][()].flatten()\n\n        for i in range(len(quality_refs)):\n            quality = h5_read_string(f, quality_refs[i]).strip().lower()\n            trialtm = f[trialtm_refs[i]][()].flatten().astype(np.float64)\n            trial = f[trial_refs[i]][()].flatten().astype(np.float64)\n            all_qualities.append(quality)\n            all_spike_times.append(trialtm)\n            all_spike_trials.append(trial)\n\n    # --- traj ---\n    traj_data = None\n    try:\n        traj_refs = obj['traj'][()].flatten()\n        traj_data = {'views': []}\n        for view_idx in range(len(traj_refs)):\n            traj_group = f[traj_refs[view_idx]]\n            ts_refs = traj_group['ts'][()].flatten()\n            ft_refs = traj_group['frameTimes'][()].flatten()\n            feat_names_refs = traj_group['featNames'][()].flatten()\n\n            # Read feat names from first trial\n            feat_refs_trial = f[feat_names_refs[0]][()].flatten()\n            feat_names = [h5_read_string(f, fr) for fr in feat_refs_trial]\n\n            view_trials = []\n            for t in range(min(ntrials, len(ts_refs))):\n                try:\n                    ts = f[ts_refs[t]][()].astype(np.float64)  # (nFeats, 3, nFrames) in HDF5\n                    ft = f[ft_refs[t]][()].flatten().astype(np.float64)\n                    view_trials.append({'ts': ts, 'frameTimes': ft, 'ts_format': 'hdf5'})\n                except Exception:\n                    view_trials.append(None)\n            traj_data['views'].append({'trials': view_trials, 'feat_names': feat_names})\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n\n    f.close()\n\n    return {\n        'bp': bp, 'vidshift': vidshift, 'ntrials': ntrials,\n        'all_spike_times': all_spike_times, 'all_spike_trials': all_spike_trials,\n        'all_qualities': all_qualities, 'traj_data': traj_data,\n        'n_units_raw': len(all_qualities),\n    }\n\n\ndef load_session(anm, date, probes, data_dir_key, params):\n    \"\"\"\n    Load and process one session, following the reference pipeline:\n    loadObjs -> findClusters -> alignSpikes -> getSeq -> removeLowFRClusters\n    Also loads motion energy and computes kinematic features.\n    \"\"\"\n    t0 = time.time()\n    data_dir = DATA_DIRS[data_dir_key]\n    data_fn = os.path.join(data_dir, f'data_structure_{anm}_{date}.mat')\n    me_fn = os.path.join(data_dir, f'motionEnergy_{anm}_{date}.mat')\n\n    session_id = f'{anm}_{date}'\n\n    if not os.path.exists(data_fn):\n        print(f\"  WARNING: {data_fn} not found, skipping\")\n        return None\n\n    # Detect format and load raw data\n    fmt = _detect_file_format(data_fn)\n    try:\n        if fmt == 'hdf5':\n            raw = _load_raw_data_hdf5(data_fn, probes)\n        else:\n            raw = _load_raw_data_v5(data_fn, probes)\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"  WARNING: Could not load {session_id}: {e}\")\n        return None\n\n    bp = raw['bp']\n    ntrials = raw['ntrials']\n    vidshift = raw['vidshift']\n    all_spike_times = raw['all_spike_times']\n    all_spike_trials = raw['all_spike_trials']\n    all_qualities = raw['all_qualities']\n    traj_data = raw['traj_data']\n    n_units_before_quality = raw['n_units_raw']\n    ev = bp['ev']\n\n    # --- Filter clusters by quality ---\n    quality_exclude = params['quality_exclude']\n    keep_mask = np.array([q not in quality_exclude and q != '' and q != 'nan'\n                          for q in all_qualities])\n    good_indices = np.where(keep_mask)[0]\n\n    if len(good_indices) == 0:\n        print(f\"  WARNING: {session_id} has no units after quality filter, skipping\")\n        return None\n\n    # --- Trial filtering ---\n    trial_mask = (bp['early'] == 0) & (bp['stim_enable'] == 0)\n    valid_trials = np.where(trial_mask)[0] + 1  # 1-indexed\n    if len(valid_trials) < 2:\n        print(f\"  WARNING: {session_id} has < 2 valid trials, skipping\")\n        return None\n\n    # --- Align spikes to goCue and bin ---\n    align_times = ev[params['align_event']]\n    edges = np.arange(params['tmin'], params['tmax'] + params['dt'], params['dt'])\n    time_axis = edges[:-1] + params['dt'] / 2\n    n_timebins = len(time_axis)\n    n_valid_trials = len(valid_trials)\n\n    trialdat = np.zeros((len(good_indices), n_timebins, n_valid_trials), dtype=np.float32)\n\n    for neuron_idx, clu_idx in enumerate(good_indices):\n        spike_tm = all_spike_times[clu_idx]\n        spike_trial = all_spike_trials[clu_idx]\n\n        for t_idx, trial_num in enumerate(valid_trials):\n            spk_mask = spike_trial == trial_num\n            if not np.any(spk_mask):\n                continue\n            spk_times = spike_tm[spk_mask] - align_times[trial_num - 1]\n            counts, _ = np.histogram(spk_times, bins=edges)\n            fr = causal_gaussian_smooth(counts.astype(np.float64) / params['dt'],\n                                       params['smooth_window'],\n                                       params['smooth_bctype'])\n            trialdat[neuron_idx, :, t_idx] = fr.astype(np.float32)\n\n    # --- Remove low FR clusters ---\n    mean_fr = np.mean(trialdat, axis=(1, 2))\n    fr_mask = mean_fr > params['low_fr']\n    trialdat = trialdat[fr_mask, :, :]\n    n_neurons = trialdat.shape[0]\n\n    if n_neurons < params['min_units']:\n        print(f\"  WARNING: {session_id} has {n_neurons} units (< {params['min_units']}), skipping\")\n        return None\n\n    # --- Extract trial-level variables ---\n    valid_trial_indices = valid_trials - 1\n    lick_direction = bp['R'][valid_trial_indices].copy()\n    behavioral_context = 1.0 - bp['autowater'][valid_trial_indices]\n    outcome = bp['hit'][valid_trial_indices].copy()\n\n    # --- Load Motion Energy ---\n    me_data = None\n    if os.path.exists(me_fn):\n        try:\n            me_data = _load_motion_energy_generic(me_fn, traj_data, vidshift,\n                                                  align_times, time_axis, ntrials)\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"  WARNING: Could not load motion energy for {session_id}: {e}\")\n\n    # --- Compute tongue and paw velocity from DLC ---\n    tongue_vel = _compute_feature_velocity_generic(\n        traj_data, ntrials, view=2, feat_name='top_tongue',\n        vidshift=vidshift, align_times=align_times,\n        time_axis=time_axis, is_tongue=True)\n\n    paw_vel = _compute_feature_velocity_generic(\n        traj_data, ntrials, view=2, feat_name='top_paw',\n        vidshift=vidshift, align_times=align_times,\n        time_axis=time_axis, is_tongue=False)\n\n    # --- Subsample to valid trials ---\n    valid_0idx = valid_trial_indices  # 0-indexed\n\n    if tongue_vel is not None:\n        tongue_vel = tongue_vel[:, valid_0idx]\n    if paw_vel is not None:\n        paw_vel = paw_vel[:, valid_0idx]\n    if me_data is not None:\n        me_data = me_data[:, valid_0idx]\n\n    # --- Discretize continuous outputs (50th percentile per session) ---\n    tongue_vel_disc = _discretize_velocity(tongue_vel, n_timebins, n_valid_trials)\n    paw_vel_disc = _discretize_velocity(paw_vel, n_timebins, n_valid_trials)\n    me_disc = _discretize_velocity(me_data, n_timebins, n_valid_trials)\n\n    elapsed = time.time() - t0\n    print(f\"  {session_id}: {n_neurons} neurons, {n_valid_trials} trials, \"\n          f\"{n_units_before_quality} raw units -> {int(keep_mask.sum())} after quality -> \"\n          f\"{n_neurons} after FR filter ({elapsed:.1f}s)\")\n\n    return {\n        'session_id': session_id,\n        'animal': anm,\n        'date': date,\n        'n_neurons': n_neurons,\n        'n_trials': n_valid_trials,\n        'time_axis': time_axis,\n        'trialdat': trialdat,  # (n_neurons, n_timebins, n_trials)\n        'lick_direction': lick_direction,\n        'behavioral_context': behavioral_context,\n        'outcome': outcome,\n        'tongue_vel_disc': tongue_vel_disc,  # (n_timebins, n_trials)\n        'paw_vel_disc': paw_vel_disc,\n        'me_disc': me_disc,\n        'tongue_vel_raw': tongue_vel,\n        'paw_vel_raw': paw_vel,\n        'me_raw': me_data,\n    }\n\n\ndef _load_motion_energy_generic(me_fn, traj_data, vidshift, align_times, time_axis, ntrials):\n    \"\"\"Load motion energy and align to neural time axis following loadMotionEnergy.m\"\"\"\n    import scipy.io as sio\n\n    me_cell = None\n    try:\n        me_file = sio.loadmat(me_fn, squeeze_me=True)\n        me_struct = me_file['me']\n        me_cell = me_struct['data'].item()\n    except Exception:\n        me_h5 = h5py.File(me_fn, 'r')\n        me_grp = me_h5['me']\n        me_data_refs = me_grp['data'][()].flatten()\n        me_cell = [me_h5[ref][()].flatten().astype(np.float64) for ref in me_data_refs]\n        me_h5.close()\n\n    side_cam = traj_data['views'][0] if traj_data is not None else None\n    n_time = len(time_axis)\n    me_aligned = np.full((n_time, ntrials), np.nan, dtype=np.float32)\n\n    for trix in range(ntrials):\n        try:\n            me_trial = me_cell[trix].flatten().astype(np.float64) if hasattr(me_cell[trix], 'flatten') else np.array([float(me_cell[trix])])\n        except (IndexError, TypeError):\n            continue\n        if me_trial.size == 0:\n            continue\n\n        ft = None\n        if side_cam is not None and trix < len(side_cam['trials']) and side_cam['trials'][trix] is not None:\n            ft = side_cam['trials'][trix]['frameTimes']\n        if ft is None or ft.size == 0 or np.all(np.isnan(ft)):\n            ft = np.arange(1, me_trial.size + 1) / 400.0\n\n        ft_aligned = ft - vidshift - align_times[trix]\n        min_len = min(len(ft_aligned), len(me_trial))\n        ft_aligned, me_trial = ft_aligned[:min_len], me_trial[:min_len]\n        valid = ~np.isnan(ft_aligned) & ~np.isnan(me_trial)\n        if valid.sum() < 2:\n            continue\n        try:\n            me_aligned[:, trix] = interp1d(ft_aligned[valid], me_trial[valid],\n                                           kind='linear', bounds_error=False,\n                                           fill_value=np.nan)(time_axis).astype(np.float32)\n        except Exception:\n            continue\n\n    for trix in range(ntrials):\n        col = me_aligned[:, trix]\n        if not np.isnan(col).all() and np.isnan(col).any():\n            me_aligned[:, trix] = _fill_nearest(col)\n\n    return me_aligned\n\n\ndef _compute_feature_velocity_generic(traj_data, ntrials, view, feat_name, vidshift, align_times, time_axis, is_tongue=False):\n    \"\"\"Compute velocity of a DLC feature from traj_data (format-agnostic).\"\"\"\n    n_time = len(time_axis)\n    speed = np.full((n_time, ntrials), np.nan, dtype=np.float32)\n\n    if traj_data is None:\n        return None\n\n    view_idx = view - 1\n    if view_idx >= len(traj_data['views']):\n        return None\n\n    view_data = traj_data['views'][view_idx]\n    feat_names = view_data['feat_names']\n\n    feat_idx = None\n    for idx, fn in enumerate(feat_names):\n        if fn == feat_name:\n            feat_idx = idx\n            break\n    if feat_idx is None:\n        for idx, fn in enumerate(feat_names):\n            if feat_name in fn:\n                feat_idx = idx\n                break\n    if feat_idx is None:\n        return None\n\n    for trix in range(min(ntrials, len(view_data['trials']))):\n        trial_info = view_data['trials'][trix]\n        if trial_info is None:\n            continue\n        try:\n            ts = trial_info['ts']\n            ft = trial_info['frameTimes']\n            ts_fmt = trial_info['ts_format']\n\n            if ts.ndim != 3:\n                continue\n\n            if ts_fmt == 'hdf5':\n                x_raw = ts[feat_idx, 0, :]\n                y_raw = ts[feat_idx, 1, :]\n            else:\n                x_raw = ts[:, 0, feat_idx]\n                y_raw = ts[:, 1, feat_idx]\n\n            if ft.size == 0 or np.all(np.isnan(ft)):\n                ft = np.arange(1, len(x_raw) + 1) / 400.0\n\n            ft_aligned = ft - vidshift - align_times[trix]\n            min_len = min(len(ft_aligned), len(x_raw))\n            ft_a, x_r, y_r = ft_aligned[:min_len], x_raw[:min_len], y_raw[:min_len]\n\n            valid = ~np.isnan(ft_a) & ~np.isnan(x_r) & ~np.isnan(y_r)\n            if valid.sum() < 2:\n                continue\n\n            xpos = interp1d(ft_a[valid], x_r[valid], kind='linear',\n                            bounds_error=False, fill_value=np.nan)(time_axis)\n            ypos = interp1d(ft_a[valid], y_r[valid], kind='linear',\n                            bounds_error=False, fill_value=np.nan)(time_axis)\n\n            if not is_tongue:\n                xpos = _fill_nearest(xpos)\n                ypos = _fill_nearest(ypos)\n\n            xvel = np.gradient(xpos)\n            yvel = np.gradient(ypos)\n\n            if is_tongue:\n                xvel[np.isnan(xvel)] = 0\n                yvel[np.isnan(yvel)] = 0\n            else:\n                base_xvel = np.nanmedian(np.diff(xpos))\n                base_yvel = np.nanmedian(np.diff(ypos))\n                xvel -= (base_xvel if not np.isnan(base_xvel) else 0)\n                yvel -= (base_yvel if not np.isnan(base_yvel) else 0)\n                xvel = _fill_nearest(xvel)\n                yvel = _fill_nearest(yvel)\n\n            speed[:, trix] = np.sqrt(xvel**2 + yvel**2).astype(np.float32)\n        except Exception:\n            continue\n\n    return speed\n\n\ndef _fill_nearest(arr):\n    \"\"\"Fill NaN values with nearest non-NaN value.\"\"\"\n    nans = np.isnan(arr)\n    if not nans.any():\n        return arr\n    if nans.all():\n        return arr\n    valid_idx = np.where(~nans)[0]\n    nan_idx = np.where(nans)[0]\n    nearest = np.searchsorted(valid_idx, nan_idx).clip(0, len(valid_idx) - 1)\n    arr[nans] = arr[valid_idx[nearest]]\n    return arr\n\n\ndef _discretize_velocity(data, n_timebins, n_trials):\n    \"\"\"Discretize continuous data into 2 bins using 50th percentile per session.\"\"\"\n    if data is None:\n        return np.zeros((n_timebins, n_trials), dtype=np.int64)\n\n    # Compute 50th percentile across all valid (non-NaN) values\n    valid_vals = data[~np.isnan(data)]\n    if valid_vals.size == 0:\n        return np.zeros((n_timebins, n_trials), dtype=np.int64)\n\n    threshold = np.percentile(valid_vals, 50)\n    disc = (data >= threshold).astype(np.int64)\n    # Replace NaN entries with 0\n    disc[np.isnan(data)] = 0\n    return disc\n\n\n# =============================================================================\n# Plotting functions\n# =============================================================================\n\ndef plot_processing(session_data, save_path):\n    \"\"\"Plot processing visualizations for one session.\"\"\"\n    fig, axes = plt.subplots(4, 3, figsize=(20, 16))\n    session_id = session_data['session_id']\n    fig.suptitle(f'Processing: {session_id}', fontsize=14)\n\n    time_axis = session_data['time_axis']\n    trialdat = session_data['trialdat']\n    n_neurons, n_time, n_trials = trialdat.shape\n\n    # Pick a sample trial\n    trial_idx = min(5, n_trials - 1)\n\n    # 1. Neural activity heatmap for sample trial\n    ax = axes[0, 0]\n    vmax = np.percentile(trialdat[:, :, trial_idx], 95)\n    ax.imshow(trialdat[:, :, trial_idx], aspect='auto', cmap='viridis',\n              extent=[time_axis[0], time_axis[-1], n_neurons, 0], vmin=0, vmax=max(vmax, 1))\n    ax.axvline(0, color='r', linestyle='--', alpha=0.7)\n    ax.set_title(f'Neural activity (trial {trial_idx})')\n    ax.set_xlabel('Time from goCue (s)')\n    ax.set_ylabel('Neuron')\n\n    # 2. --- Build target format ---\n    # Subjects\n    all_animals = sorted(set(s['animal'] for s in all_sessions))\n    animal_to_idx = {a: i for i, a in enumerate(all_animals)}\n\n    # Time axis (should be same for all)\n    time_axis = all_sessions[0]['time_axis']\n    n_timebins = len(time_axis)\n\n    # Build data lists\n    neural = []\n    inputs = []\n    outputs = []\n    subject_idx = []\n    brain_region_idx = []\n\n    for sess in all_sessions:\n        n_neurons = sess['n_neurons']\n        n_trials = sess['n_trials']\n        trialdat = sess['trialdat']  # (n_neurons, n_timebins, n_trials)\n\n        # Neural: list of trials, each (n_neurons, n_timebins)\n        sess_neural = []\n        sess_input = []\n        sess_output = []\n\n        for t in range(n_trials):\n            # Neural\n            sess_neural.append(trialdat[:, :, t].astype(np.float32))\n\n            # Input: time from goCue (continuous, time-varying)\n            # Shape: (1, n_timebins)\n            time_input = time_axis.astype(np.float32).reshape(1, -1)\n            sess_input.append(time_input)\n\n            # Output: per-trial variables broadcast + time-varying variables\n            # Per-trial: lick_direction, behavioral_context, outcome\n            # Time-varying: tongue_vel_disc, paw_vel_disc, me_disc\n            lick_dir = np.full((1, n_timebins), int(sess['lick_direction'][t]), dtype=np.int64)\n            context = np.full((1, n_timebins), int(sess['behavioral_context'][t]), dtype=np.int64)\n            outc = np.full((1, n_timebins), int(sess['outcome'][t]), dtype=np.int64)\n            tongue_v = sess['tongue_vel_disc'][:, t].astype(np.int64).reshape(1, -1)\n            paw_v = sess['paw_vel_disc'][:, t].astype(np.int64).reshape(1, -1)\n            me_v = sess['me_disc'][:, t].astype(np.int64).reshape(1, -1)\n\n            trial_output = np.concatenate([lick_dir, context, outc, tongue_v, paw_v, me_v], axis=0).astype(np.int64)\n            sess_output.append(trial_output)\n\n        neural.append(sess_neural)\n        inputs.append(sess_input)\n        outputs.append(sess_output)\n        subject_idx.append(animal_to_idx[sess['animal']])\n\n        # Brain region: all ALM\n        brain_region_idx.append(np.zeros(n_neurons, dtype=np.int64))\n\n    data = {\n        'neural': neural,\n        'input': inputs,\n        'output': outputs,\n        'subjects': all_animals,\n        'subject_idx': np.array(subject_idx, dtype=np.int64),\n        'brain_regions': ['ALM'],\n        'brain_region_idx': brain_region_idx,\n        'input_names': ['time_from_go_cue'],\n        'output_names': ['lick_direction', 'behavioral_context', 'outcome',\n                         'tongue_velocity', 'paw_velocity', 'motion_energy'],\n        'output_values': [\n            ['left', 'right'],        # lick_direction\n            ['WC', 'DR'],             # behavioral_context\n            ['incorrect', 'correct'], # outcome\n            ['low', 'high'],          # tongue_velocity\n            ['low', 'high'],          # paw_velocity\n            ['low', 'high'],          # motion_energy\n        ],\n        'metadata': {\n            'task_description': 'Two-context delayed-response and water-cued licking task with ALM recordings',\n            'time_bin_size': PARAMS['dt'] * 1000,  # in ms\n            'temporal_alignment_event': 'go cue onset',\n            'off_start': PARAMS['tmin'],\n            'off_end': PARAMS['tmax'],\n            'smooth_window': PARAMS['smooth_window'],\n            'smooth_type': 'causal_gaussian',\n            'low_fr_threshold': PARAMS['low_fr'],\n            'min_units_per_session': PARAMS['min_units'],\n            'quality_excluded': list(PARAMS['quality_exclude']),\n            'source_paper': 'Hasnain, Birnbaum et al., Nature Neuroscience 2024',\n            'n_sessions': len(all_sessions),\n            'n_subjects': len(all_animals),\n        }\n    }\n\n    # Save\n    print(f\"\\nSaving to {outfile}...\")\n    with open(outfile, 'wb') as pkl:\n        pickle.dump(data, pkl, protocol=4)\n    file_size = os.path.getsize(outfile) / (1024 * 1024)\n    print(f\"Saved {outfile} ({file_size:.1f} MB)\")\n    print(f\"Total time: {time.time() - total_t0:.1f}s\")\n\n    # Print summary\n    total_neurons = sum(len(br) for br in brain_region_idx)\n    total_trials = sum(len(s) for s in neural)\n    print(f\"\\nSummary:\")\n    print(f\"  Sessions: {len(all_sessions)}\")\n    print(f\"  Subjects: {len(all_animals)}\")\n    print(f\"  Total neurons: {total_neurons}\")\n    print(f\"  Total trials: {total_trials}\")\n    print(f\"  Mean neurons/session: {total_neurons / len(all_sessions):.1f}\")\n    print(f\"  Mean trials/session: {total_trials / len(all_sessions):.1f}\")\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description='Convert Hasnain et al. data to decoder format')\n    parser.add_argument('outfile', type=str, help='Output pickle file path')\n    parser.add_argument('--full', action='store_true', default=True, help='Process all sessions (default)')\n    parser.add_argument('--sample', action='store_true', help='Process only 2 sessions for testing')\n    parser.add_argument('--show-processing', action='store_true', help='Plot processing visualizations')\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    if args.sample:\n        # Methods |\n| Sample tone duration | 1.3 s | Methods |\n\n### Processing Details\n- **Temporal alignment**: Align to goCue onset\n- **Time window**: -2.5 to 2.5 s from goCue\n- **Spike binning**: 10ms bins, histc with edges tmin:dt:tmax\n- **Smoothing**: Causal Gaussian kernel, window=15 (via mySmooth)\n- **Firing rates**: spks/sec (spike count / dt)\n- **Video offset**: subtract 0.5s from frameTimes (or use findVideoOffset via bitcode)\n- **Motion energy alignment**: Interpolate to neural time axis using interp1, align to goCue\n- **Kinematics**: DLC position -> velocity via gradient, fill missing values\n\n### Curation Steps\n\n**Neuron curation rules**:\n1. Quality filter: Exclude 'garbage', 'noisy', 'gabrga', 'real?' (quality='all' mode)\n2. Low firing rate: Remove neurons with mean FR < 1 Hz across all trials\n3. Min units: Sessions with < 10 units excluded\n\n**Trial curation rules**:\n1. Exclude early lick trials (~early)\n2. Exclude stim/photoinactivation trials (~stim.enable)\n3. For hit analyses: use hit trials only\n4. For DR: ~autowater (autowater=0)\n5. For WC: autowater=1\n\n### Decoders Trained (in paper)\n| Decoded variable | Method | Accuracy |\n|-----------------|--------|----------|\n| Choice (L vs R) | Logistic regression, ridge, 4-fold CV | Shown in Fig 3b (time-varying, ~0.7-0.9 during delay/response) |\n| Context (DR vs WC) | Logistic regression, ridge, 4-fold CV | Shown in Fig 4b (time-varying, ~0.6-0.85) |\n| Choice from kinematics | SVM, 75ms bins, 4-fold CV | Shown in Fig 3b |\n| Context from kinematics | SVM, 75ms bins, 4-fold CV | Shown in Fig 4b |\n\n---\n\n## Step 4: Check for Consistency\n**Status**: COMPLETE\n\n### Discrepancies Found\n| Topic | Code says | Data shows | Paper says | Resolution |\n|-------|-----------|------------|------------|------------|\n| DR sessions | 50 in loading scripts | 25 data files in Ephys | 25 sessions, 9 mice | JEB4/JEB5 have loading scripts but no data files. - matches reference\n6. **Include ALL sessions** (both Ephys_Behavior and RandomizedDelay) that have loading scripts\n7. **Brain region**: All ALM - single brain region\n8. **Dual probe sessions**: Concatenate neurons from both probes (both are ALM)\n9. **Trial filtering for decoder**: Include ALL trial types (hit, miss, no-response) per session. The decoder should predict outcome, so needs all types.\n10. **Exclude early lick and stim trials**: Per reference code conditions (~early, ~stim.enable)\n11. **Tongue velocity**: Compute from bottom cam DLC features (top_tongue or similar). Use Euclidean velocity = sqrt(vx^2 + vy^2)\n12. **Paw velocity**: From bottom cam (top_paw, bottom_paw). Use Euclidean velocity\n13. **Motion energy**: Load from motionEnergy files, interpolate to neural time axis\n14. **Per-session discretization**: 50th percentile threshold computed on all timepoints across all trials in session\n\n### Planned Sanity Checks\n- [ ] Verify neuron count after filtering matches paper (~1651 for DR, ~845 for randomized)\n- [ ] Verify number of sessions matches paper {\n    37→    'neural':           list of 44 sessions, each a list of trials, each (n_neurons, 500) float32\n    38→    'input':            list of 44 sessions, each a list of trials, each (1, 500) Load MATLAB data objects (HDF5 and v5 formats)\n    55→2. Quality filter neurons (exclude garbage, noisy, gabrga, real?)\n    56→3. Filter trials (exclude early lick and photostimulation)\n    57→4. Align spikes to go cue onset\n    58→5. Bin at 10ms, smooth with causal Gaussian kernel (window=15)\n    59→6. Convert to firing rate (spks/sec)\n    60→7.  126→\n   127→### Processing Details\n   128→- **Temporal alignment**: Align to goCue onset\n   129→- **Time window**: -2.5 to 2.5 s from goCue\n   130→- **Spike binning**: 10ms bins, histc with edges gradient, fill missing values\n   136→\n   137→### Curation Steps\n   138→\n   139→**Neuron curation rules**:\n   140→1. Quality filter: Exclude 'garbage', 'noisy', 'gabrga', 'real?' (quality='all' mode)\n   141→2. Low firing rate: Remove neurons with mean FR < 1 Hz across all trials\n   142→3. Min units: Sessions with < 10 units excluded\n   143→\n   144→**Trial curation rules**:\n   145→1. Exclude early lick trials (~early)\n   146→2. Exclude stim/photoinactivation trials (~stim.enable)\n   147→3. For hit analyses: use hit trials only\n   148→4. For DR: ~autowater (autowater=0)\n   149→5. For WC: autowater=1\n   150→\n   151→### Decoders Trained (in paper)\n   152→| Decoded variable | Method | Accuracy |\n   153→|-----------------|--------|----------|\n   154→| Choice (L vs R) | Logistic regression, ridge, 4-fold CV | Shown in Fig 3b (time-varying, ~0.7-0.9 during delay/response) |\n   155→| Context (DR vs WC) | Logistic regression, ridge, 4-fold CV | Shown in Fig 4b (time-varying, ~0.6-0.85) |\n   156→| Choice from kinematics | SVM, 75ms bins, 4-fold CV | Shown in Fig 3b |\n   157→| Context from kinematics | SVM, 75ms bins, 4-fold CV | Shown in Fig 4b |\n   158→\n   159→---\n   160→\n   161→## Step 4: Check for Consistency\n   162→**Status**: COMPLETE\n   163→\n   164→### Discrepancies Found\n   165→| Topic | Code says | Data shows | Paper says | Resolution |\n   166→|-------|-----------|------------|------------|------------|\n   167→| DR sessions | 50 in loading scripts | 25 data files in Ephys | 25 sessions, 9 mice | JEB4/JEB5 have loading scripts but no data files. | Linear ramp from -2.5 to 2.5s |\n   184→| obj.bp.R/L | output[0]: lick_direction | L=0, R=1, per-trial | - | From trial info |\n   185→| obj.bp.autowater | output[1]: behavioral_context | WC=0 -> autowater=1 maps to DR=0?, No: WC=0, DR=1 | - | autowater=1 -> WC=0, autowater=0 -> DR=1 |\n   186→| obj.bp.hit | output[2]: outcome | incorrect=0, correct=1, per-trial | - | hit=1 -> correct=1, miss=1 -> incorrect=0 |\n   187→| DLC tongue velocity | output[3]: tongue_velocity | Discretize 50th percentile per session | findVelocity, getKinematicsFromVideo | Time-varying, from bottom cam |\n   188→| DLC paw velocity | output[4]: paw_velocity | Discretize 50th percentile per session | findVelocity, getKinematicsFromVideo | Time-varying, from bottom cam |\n   189→| Motion energy | output[5]: motion_energy | Discretize 50th percentile per session | loadMotionEnergy | Time-varying, from motionEnergy file |\n   190→\n   191→### Key Decisions\n   192→1. **Align to goCue**: As specified in decoder task and paper default\n   193→2. **10ms time bins**: Matches WorkingWithDataObjs.m tutorial and paper\n   194→3. **Causal Gaussian smooth, window=15**: Matches reference code\n   195→4. **lowFR=1 Hz**: Matches paper and tutorial\n   196→5. **Quality='all'**: Exclude garbage, noisy, gabrga, real? - matches reference\n   197→6. **Include ALL sessions** (both Ephys_Behavior and RandomizedDelay) that have loading scripts\n   198→7. **Brain region**: All ALM - single brain region\n   199→8. **Dual probe sessions**: Concatenate neurons from both probes (both are ALM)\n   200→9. **Trial filtering for decoder**: Include ALL trial types (hit, miss, no-response) per session. The decoder should predict outcome, so needs all types.\n   201→10. **Exclude early lick and stim trials**: Per reference code conditions (~early, ~stim.enable)\n   202→11. **Tongue velocity**: Compute from bottom cam DLC features (top_tongue or similar). Use Euclidean velocity = sqrt(vx^2 + vy^2)\n   203→12. **Paw velocity**: From bottom cam (top_paw, bottom_paw). Use Euclidean velocity\n   204→13. **Motion energy**: Load from motionEnergy files, interpolate to neural time axis\n   205→14. **Per-session discretization**: 50th percentile threshold computed on all timepoints across all trials in session\n   206→\n   207→### Planned Sanity Checks\n   208→- [ ] Verify neuron count after filtering matches paper (~1651 for DR, ~845 for randomized)\n   209→- [ ] 'input':            list of 44 sessions, each a list of trials, each (1, 500) float32\n    'output':           list of 44 sessions, each a list of trials, each (6, 500) int64\n    'input_names':      ['time_from_go_cue']\n    'output_names':     ['lick_direction', 'behavioral_context', 'outcome',\n                         'tongue_velocity', 'paw_velocity', 'motion_energy']\n    'output_values': parameters\n}\n```\n\n## Processing Pipeline\n1. Load MATLAB data objects (HDF5 and v5 formats)\n2. Quality filter neurons (exclude garbage, noisy, gabrga, real?)\n3. Filter trials (exclude early lick and photostimulation)\n4. Align spikes to go cue onset\n5. Bin at 10ms, smooth with causal Gaussian kernel (window=15)\n6. Convert to firing rate (spks/sec)\n7. Remove neurons with mean FR < 1 Hz\n8. Extract trial variables (lick direction, context, outcome)\n9. Compute tongue and paw velocity from DLC trajectories\n10. Load and align motion energy\n11. array of references\n   115→            return ''.join(chr(int(f[v][()].flat[0])) for v in val.flat)\n   116→        if val.dtype.kind in ('U', 'S'):\n   117→            return str(val.flat[0])\n   118→        # numeric array that represents characters (uint16)\n   119→        if val.dtype.kind in ('u', 'i') and val.size < 200:\n   120→            try:\n   121→                return ''.join(chr(int(c)) for c in val.flat)\n   122→            except (ValueError, OverflowError):\n   123→                pass\n   124→    if isinstance(val, (np.integer, np.floating)):\n   125→        return str(val)\n   126→    return str(val)\n   127→\n   128→\n   129→def h5_read_array(f, ref_or_dataset):\n   130→    \"\"\"Read a numeric array from HDF5, handling object references.\"\"\"\n   131→    if isinstance(ref_or_dataset, h5py.h5r.Reference):\n   132→        ds = f[ref_or_dataset]\n   133→    else:\n   134→        ds = ref_or_dataset\n   135→    val = ds[()]\n   136→    if isinstance(val, np.ndarray):\n   137→        return val.astype(np.float64)\n   138→    return np.array([float(val)])\n   139→\n   140→\n   141→def h5_read_cell_array_of_arrays(f, dataset):\n   142→    \"\"\"Read a MATLAB cell array of numeric arrays from HDF5.\"\"\"\n   143→    refs = dataset[()]\n   144→    result = []\n   145→    for ref in refs.flat:\n   146→        arr = h5_read_array(f, ref)\n   147→        result.append(arr)\n   148→    return result\n   149→\n   150→\n   151→# =============================================================================\n   152→# Signal processing functions (matching MATLAB reference code)\n   153→# =============================================================================\n   154→\n   155→def causal_gaussian_smooth(x, N, bctype='reflect'):\n   156→    \"\"\"\n   157→    Replicate mySmooth.m: causal Gaussian smoothing.\n   158→    x: (time,) or (time, features) array\n   159→    N: window size\n   160→    bctype: 'reflect', 'zeropad', or 'none'\n   161→    \"\"\"\n   162→    if N <= 1:\n   163→        return x\n   164→\n   165→    was_1d = (x.ndim == 1)\n   166→    if was_1d:\n   167→        x = x[:, np.newaxis]\n   168→\n   169→    # Build kernel\n   170→    kern = np.array(sig_windows.gaussian(N, std=(N - 1) / (2 * 2.5)))\n   171→    # Make causal: zero out first half\n   172→    kern[:N // 2] = 0\n   173→    kern = kern / kern.sum()\n   174→\n   175→    # Handle boundary conditions\n   176→    if bctype == 'reflect':\n   177→        x_filt = np.concatenate([x[:N, :], x], axis=0)\n   178→        trim = N\n   179→    elif bctype == 'zeropad':\n   180→        x_filt = np.concatenate([np.zeros((N, x.shape[1])), x], axis=0)\n   181→        trim = N\n   182→    else:\n   183→        x_filt = x\n   184→        trim = 0\n   185→\n   186→    # Convolve each column\n   187→    out = np.zeros_like(x_filt)\n   188→    for j in range(x_filt.shape[1]):\n   189→        out[:, j] = np.convolve(x_filt[:, j], kern, mode='same')\n   190→    out = out[trim:, :]\n   191→\n   192→    if was_1d:\n   193→        return out[:, 0]\n   194→    return out\n   195→\n   196→\n   197→# =============================================================================\n   198→# Data loading functions\n   199→# =============================================================================\n   200→\n   201→def _detect_file_format(data_fn):\n   202→    \"\"\"Detect if a .mat file is HDF5 (v7.3) or v5 format.\"\"\"\n   203→    try:\n   204→        f = h5py.File(data_fn, 'r')\n   205→        f.close()\n   206→        return 'hdf5'\n   207→    except Exception:\n   208→        return 'v5'\n   209→\n   210→\n   211→def _load_raw_data_v5(data_fn, probes):\n   212→    \"\"\"Load raw data from MATLAB v5 format file. Returns common dict format.\"\"\"\n   213→    import scipy.io as sio\n   214→    mat = sio.loadmat(data_fn, squeeze_me=False)\n   215→    obj = mat['obj']\n   216→\n   217→    # --- bp ---\n   218→    bp_raw = obj['bp'][0, 0]\n   219→    bp = {}\n   220→    bp['Ntrials'] = int(bp_raw['Ntrials'][0, 0].flat[0])\n   221→    ntrials = bp['Ntrials']\n   222→\n   223→    for field in ['hit', 'miss', 'no', 'early', 'L', 'R', 'autowater']:\n   224→        bp[field] = bp_raw[field][0, 0].flatten().astype(np.float64)[:ntrials]\n   225→\n   226→    stim_enable = np.zeros(ntrials)\n   227→    if 'stim' in bp_raw.dtype.names:\n   228→        stim_raw = bp_raw['stim'][0, 0]\n   229→        if 'enable' in stim_raw.dtype.names:\n   230→            stim_enable = stim_raw['enable'][0, 0].flatten().astype(np.float64)[:ntrials]\n   231→    bp['stim_enable'] = stim_enable\n   232→\n   233→    ev = {}\n   234→    ev_raw = bp_raw['ev'][0, 0]\n   235→    for field in ['goCue', 'sample', 'delay']:\n   236→        ev[field] = ev_raw[field][0, 0].flatten().astype(np.float64)[:ntrials]\n   237→    bp['ev'] = ev\n   238→\n   239→    # --- vidshift ---\n   240→    vidshift = 0.5\n   241→    try:\n   242→        bit_start_arr = ev_raw['bitStart'][0, 0].flatten().astype(np.float64)\n   243→        bit_start = np.nanmedian(bit_start_arr)\n   244→        sglx_raw = obj['sglx'][0, 0]\n   245→        fs = sglx_raw['fs'][0, 0].flat[0]\n   246→        bitcode_raw = sglx_raw['bitcode'][0, 0]\n   247→        bitcode_bitstart = np.nanmedian(bitcode_raw['bitstart'][0, 0].flatten().astype(np.float64))\n   248→        vid_file_offset = bitcode_bitstart / fs\n   249→        vidshift = vid_file_offset - bit_start\n   250→    except Exception:\n   251→        pass\n   252→\n   253→    # --- clu ---\n   254→    clu_raw = obj['clu'][0, 0]\n   255→    all_spike_times = []\n   256→    all_spike_trials = []\n   257→    all_qualities = []\n   258→\n   259→    for probe_num in probes:\n   260→        probe_idx = probe_num - 1\n   261→        probe_data = clu_raw[0, probe_idx]\n   262→        n_units = probe_data.shape[1] if probe_data.ndim > 1 else probe_data.shape[0]\n   263→        for i in range(n_units):\n   264→            unit = probe_data[0, i] if probe_data.ndim > 1 else probe_data[i]\n   265→            quality = str(unit['quality'].flat[0]).strip().lower() if unit['quality'].size > 0 else ''\n   266→            trialtm = unit['trialtm'].flatten().astype(np.float64)\n   267→            trial = unit['trial'].flatten().astype(np.float64)\n   268→            all_qualities.append(quality)\n   269→            all_spike_times.append(trialtm)\n   270→            all_spike_trials.append(trial)\n   271→\n   272→    # --- traj (for velocity computation) ---\n   273→    traj_data = None\n   274→    try:\n   275→        traj_raw = obj['traj'][0, 0]\n   276→        traj_data = {'views': []}\n   277→        for view_idx in range(traj_raw.shape[1]):\n   278→            view_data = traj_raw[0, view_idx]\n   279→            view_trials = []\n   280→            feat_names = None\n   281→            for t in range(min(ntrials, view_data.shape[1] if view_data.ndim > 1 else view_data.shape[0])):\n   282→                trial_data = view_data[0, t] if view_data.ndim > 1 else view_data[t]\n   283→                ts = trial_data['ts'].astype(np.float64)  # (nFrames, 3, nFeats) in v5\n   284→                ft = trial_data['frameTimes'].flatten().astype(np.float64)\n   285→                if feat_names is None:\n   286→                    fn_raw = trial_data['featNames'].flatten()\n   287→                    feat_names = [str(x.flat[0]).strip() for x in fn_raw]\n   288→                view_trials.append({'ts': ts, 'frameTimes': ft, 'ts_format': 'v5'})\n   289→            traj_data['views'].append({'trials': view_trials, 'feat_names': feat_names})\n   290→    except Exception:\n   291→        pass\n   292→\n   293→    return {\n   294→        'bp': bp, 'vidshift': vidshift, 'ntrials': ntrials,\n   295→        'all_spike_times': all_spike_times, 'all_spike_trials': all_spike_trials,\n   296→        'all_qualities': all_qualities, 'traj_data': traj_data,\n   297→        'n_units_raw': len(all_qualities),\n   298→    }\n   299→\n   300→\n   301→def _load_raw_data_hdf5(data_fn, probes):\n   302→    \"\"\"Load raw data from HDF5 (v7.3) format file. Returns common dict format.\"\"\"\n   303→    f = h5py.File(data_fn, 'r')\n   304→    obj = f['obj']\n   305→\n   306→    bp = {}\n   307→    bp_group = obj['bp']\n   308→    bp['Ntrials'] = int(bp_group['Ntrials'][()].flat[0])\n   309→    ntrials = bp['Ntrials']\n   310→\n   311→    for field in ['hit', 'miss', 'no', 'early', 'L', 'R', 'autowater']:\n   312→        bp[field] = bp_group[field][()].flatten().astype(np.float64)[:ntrials]\n   313→\n   314→    stim_enable = np.zeros(ntrials)\n   315→    if 'stim' in bp_group:\n   316→        stim_grp = bp_group['stim']\n   317→        if 'enable' in stim_grp:\n   318→            stim_enable = stim_grp['enable'][()].flatten().astype(np.float64)[:ntrials]\n   319→    bp['stim_enable'] = stim_enable\n   320→\n   321→    ev = {}\n   322→    ev_group = bp_group['ev']\n   323→    for field in ['goCue', 'sample', 'delay']:\n   324→        ev[field] = ev_group[field][()].flatten().astype(np.float64)[:ntrials]\n   325→    bp['ev'] = ev\n   326→\n   327→    vidshift = 0.5\n   328→    try:\n   329→        bit_start = np.nanmedian(bp_group['ev']['bitStart'][()].flatten())\n   330→        sglx = obj['sglx']\n   331→        fs = sglx['fs'][()].flat[0]\n   332→        bitcode_bitstart = np.nanmedian(sglx['bitcode']['bitstart'][()].flatten())\n   333→        vid_file_offset = bitcode_bitstart / fs\n   334→        vidshift = vid_file_offset - bit_start\n   335→    except Exception:\n   336→        pass\n   337→\n   338→    clu_refs = obj['clu'][()].flatten()\n   339→    all_spike_times = []\n   340→    all_spike_trials = []\n   341→    all_qualities = []\n   342→\n   343→    for probe_num in probes:\n   344→        probe_idx = probe_num - 1\n   345→        probe_ref = clu_refs[probe_idx]\n   346→        probe_group = f[probe_ref]\n   347→        quality_refs = probe_group['quality'][()].flatten()\n   348→        trialtm_refs = probe_group['trialtm'][()].flatten()\n   349→        trial_refs = probe_group['trial'][()].flatten()\n   350→\n   351→        for i in range(len(quality_refs)):\n   352→            quality = h5_read_string(f, quality_refs[i]).strip().lower()\n   353→            trialtm = f[trialtm_refs[i]][()].flatten().astype(np.float64)\n   354→            trial = f[trial_refs[i]][()].flatten().astype(np.float64)\n   355→            all_qualities.append(quality)\n   356→            all_spike_times.append(trialtm)\n   357→            all_spike_trials.append(trial)\n   358→\n   359→    # --- traj ---\n   360→    traj_data = None\n   361→    try:\n   362→        traj_refs = obj['traj'][()].flatten()\n   363→        traj_data = {'views': []}\n   364→        for view_idx in range(len(traj_refs)):\n   365→            traj_group = f[traj_refs[view_idx]]\n   366→            ts_refs = traj_group['ts'][()].flatten()\n   367→            ft_refs = traj_group['frameTimes'][()].flatten()\n   368→            feat_names_refs = traj_group['featNames'][()].flatten()\n   369→\n   370→            # Read feat names from first trial\n   371→            feat_refs_trial = f[feat_names_refs[0]][()].flatten()\n   372→            feat_names = [h5_read_string(f, fr) for fr in feat_refs_trial]\n   373→\n   374→            view_trials = []\n   375→            for t in range(min(ntrials, len(ts_refs))):\n   376→                try:\n   377→                    ts = f[ts_refs[t]][()].astype(np.float64)  # (nFeats, 3, nFrames) in HDF5\n   378→                    ft = f[ft_refs[t]][()].flatten().astype(np.float64)\n   379→                    view_trials.append({'ts': ts, 'frameTimes': ft, 'ts_format': 'hdf5'})\n   380→                except Exception:\n   381→                    view_trials.append(None)\n   382→            traj_data['views'].append({'trials': view_trials, 'feat_names': feat_names})\n   383→    except Exception:\n   384→        pass\n   385→\n   386→    f.close()\n   387→\n   388→    return {\n   389→        'bp': bp, 'vidshift': vidshift, 'ntrials': ntrials,\n   390→        'all_spike_times': all_spike_times, 'all_spike_trials': all_spike_trials,\n   391→        'all_qualities': all_qualities, 'traj_data': traj_data,\n   392→        'n_units_raw': len(all_qualities),\n   393→    }\n   394→\n   395→\n   396→def load_session(anm, date, probes, data_dir_key, params):\n   397→    \"\"\"\n   398→    Load and process one session, following the reference pipeline:\n   399→    loadObjs -> findClusters -> alignSpikes -> getSeq -> removeLowFRClusters\n   400→    Also loads motion energy and computes kinematic features.\n   401→    \"\"\"\n   402→    t0 = time.time()\n   403→    data_dir = DATA_DIRS[data_dir_key]\n   404→    data_fn = os.path.join(data_dir, f'data_structure_{anm}_{date}.mat')\n   405→    me_fn = os.path.join(data_dir, f'motionEnergy_{anm}_{date}.mat')\n   406→\n   407→    session_id = f'{anm}_{date}'\n   408→\n   409→    if not os.path.exists(data_fn):\n   410→        print(f\"  WARNING: {data_fn} not found, skipping\")\n   411→        return None\n   412→\n   413→    # Detect format and load raw data\n   414→    fmt = _detect_file_format(data_fn)\n   415→    try:\n   416→        if fmt == 'hdf5':\n   417→            raw = _load_raw_data_hdf5(data_fn, probes)\n   418→        else:\n   419→            raw = _load_raw_data_v5(data_fn, probes)\n   420→    except Exception as e:\n   421→        print(f\"  WARNING: Could not load {session_id}: {e}\")\n   422→        return None\n   423→\n   424→    bp = raw['bp']\n   425→    ntrials = raw['ntrials']\n   426→    vidshift = raw['vidshift']\n   427→    all_spike_times = raw['all_spike_times']\n   428→    all_spike_trials = raw['all_spike_trials']\n   429→    all_qualities = raw['all_qualities']\n   430→    traj_data = raw['traj_data']\n   431→    n_units_before_quality = raw['n_units_raw']\n   432→    ev = bp['ev']\n   433→\n   434→    # --- Filter clusters by quality ---\n   435→    quality_exclude = params['quality_exclude']\n   436→    keep_mask = np.array([q not in quality_exclude and q != '' and q != 'nan'\n   437→                          for q in all_qualities])\n   438→    good_indices = np.where(keep_mask)[0]\n   439→\n   440→    if len(good_indices) == 0:\n   441→        print(f\"  WARNING: {session_id} has no units after quality filter, skipping\")\n   442→        return None\n   443→\n   444→    # --- Trial filtering ---\n   445→    trial_mask = (bp['early'] == 0) & (bp['stim_enable'] == 0)\n   446→    valid_trials = np.where(trial_mask)[0] + 1  # 1-indexed\n   447→    if len(valid_trials) < 2:\n   448→        print(f\"  WARNING: {session_id} has < 2 valid trials, skipping\")\n   449→        return None\n   450→\n   451→    # --- Align spikes to goCue and bin ---\n   452→    align_times = ev[params['align_event']]\n   453→    edges = np.arange(params['tmin'], params['tmax'] + params['dt'], params['dt'])\n   454→    time_axis = edges[:-1] + params['dt'] / 2\n   455→    n_timebins = len(time_axis)\n   456→    n_valid_trials = len(valid_trials)\n   457→\n   458→    trialdat = np.zeros((len(good_indices), n_timebins, n_valid_trials), dtype=np.float32)\n   459→\n   460→    for neuron_idx, clu_idx in enumerate(good_indices):\n   461→        spike_tm = all_spike_times[clu_idx]\n   462→        spike_trial = all_spike_trials[clu_idx]\n   463→\n   464→        for t_idx, trial_num in enumerate(valid_trials):\n   465→            spk_mask = spike_trial == trial_num\n   466→            if not np.any(spk_mask):\n   467→                continue\n   468→            spk_times = spike_tm[spk_mask] - align_times[trial_num - 1]\n   469→            counts, _ = np.histogram(spk_times, bins=edges)\n   470→            fr = causal_gaussian_smooth(counts.astype(np.float64) / params['dt'],\n   471→                                       params['smooth_window'],\n   472→                                       params['smooth_bctype'])\n   473→            trialdat[neuron_idx, :, t_idx] = fr.astype(np.float32)\n   474→\n   475→    # --- Remove low FR clusters ---\n   476→    mean_fr = np.mean(trialdat, axis=(1, 2))\n   477→    fr_mask = mean_fr > params['low_fr']\n   478→    trialdat = trialdat[fr_mask, :, :]\n   479→    n_neurons = trialdat.shape[0]\n   480→\n   481→    if n_neurons < params['min_units']:\n   482→        print(f\"  WARNING: {session_id} has {n_neurons} units (< {params['min_units']}), skipping\")\n   483→        return None\n   484→\n   485→    # --- Extract trial-level variables ---\n   486→    valid_trial_indices = valid_trials - 1\n   487→    lick_direction = bp['R'][valid_trial_indices].copy()\n   488→    behavioral_context = 1.0 - bp['autowater'][valid_trial_indices]\n   489→    outcome = bp['hit'][valid_trial_indices].copy()\n   490→\n   491→    # --- Load Motion Energy ---\n   492→    me_data = None\n   493→    if os.path.exists(me_fn):\n   494→        try:\n   495→            me_data = _load_motion_energy_generic(me_fn, traj_data, vidshift,\n   496→                                                  align_times, time_axis, ntrials)\n   497→        except Exception as e:\n   498→            print(f\"  WARNING: Could not load motion energy for {session_id}: {e}\")\n   499→\n   500→    # --- Compute tongue and paw velocity from DLC ---\n   501→    tongue_vel = _compute_feature_velocity_generic(\n   502→        traj_data, ntrials, view=2, feat_name='top_tongue',\n   503→        vidshift=vidshift, align_times=align_times,\n   504→        time_axis=time_axis, is_tongue=True)\n   505→\n   506→    paw_vel = _compute_feature_velocity_generic(\n   507→        traj_data, ntrials, view=2, feat_name='top_paw',\n   508→        vidshift=vidshift, align_times=align_times,\n   509→        time_axis=time_axis, is_tongue=False)\n   510→\n   511→    # --- Subsample to valid trials ---\n   512→    valid_0idx = valid_trial_indices  # 0-indexed\n   513→\n   514→    if tongue_vel is not None:\n   515→        tongue_vel = tongue_vel[:, valid_0idx]\n   516→    if paw_vel is not None:\n   517→        paw_vel = paw_vel[:, valid_0idx]\n   518→    if me_data is not None:\n   519→        me_data = me_data[:, valid_0idx]\n   520→\n   521→    # --- Discretize continuous outputs (50th percentile per session) ---\n   522→    tongue_vel_disc = _discretize_velocity(tongue_vel, n_timebins, n_valid_trials)\n   523→    paw_vel_disc = _discretize_velocity(paw_vel, n_timebins, n_valid_trials)\n   524→    me_disc = _discretize_velocity(me_data, n_timebins, n_valid_trials)\n   525→\n   526→    elapsed = time.time() - t0\n   527→    print(f\"  {session_id}: {n_neurons} neurons, {n_valid_trials} trials, \"\n   528→          f\"{n_units_before_quality} raw units -> {int(keep_mask.sum())} after quality -> \"\n   529→          f\"{n_neurons} after FR filter ({elapsed:.1f}s)\")\n   530→\n   531→    return {\n   532→        'session_id': session_id,\n   533→        'animal': anm,\n   534→        'date': date,\n   535→        'n_neurons': n_neurons,\n   536→        'n_trials': n_valid_trials,\n   537→        'time_axis': time_axis,\n   538→        'trialdat': trialdat,  # (n_neurons, n_timebins, n_trials)\n   539→        'lick_direction': lick_direction,\n   540→        'behavioral_context': behavioral_context,\n   541→        'outcome': outcome,\n   542→        'tongue_vel_disc': tongue_vel_disc,  # (n_timebins, n_trials)\n   543→        'paw_vel_disc': paw_vel_disc,\n   544→        'me_disc': me_disc,\n   545→        'tongue_vel_raw': tongue_vel,\n   546→        'paw_vel_raw': paw_vel,\n   547→        'me_raw': me_data,\n   548→    }\n   549→\n   550→\n   551→def _load_motion_energy_generic(me_fn, traj_data, vidshift, align_times, time_axis, ntrials):\n   552→    \"\"\"Load motion energy and align to neural time axis following loadMotionEnergy.m\"\"\"\n   553→    import scipy.io as sio\n   554→\n   555→    me_cell = None\n   556→    try:\n   557→        me_file = sio.loadmat(me_fn, squeeze_me=True)\n   558→        me_struct = me_file['me']\n   559→        me_cell = me_struct['data'].item()\n   560→    except Exception:\n   561→        me_h5 = h5py.File(me_fn, 'r')\n   562→        me_grp = me_h5['me']\n   563→        me_data_refs = me_grp['data'][()].flatten()\n   564→        me_cell = [me_h5[ref][()].flatten().astype(np.float64) for ref in me_data_refs]\n   565→        me_h5.close()\n   566→\n   567→    side_cam = traj_data['views'][0] if traj_data is not None else None\n   568→    n_time = len(time_axis)\n   569→    me_aligned = np.full((n_time, ntrials), np.nan, dtype=np.float32)\n   570→\n   571→    for trix in range(ntrials):\n   572→        try:\n   573→            me_trial = me_cell[trix].flatten().astype(np.float64) if hasattr(me_cell[trix], 'flatten') else np.array([float(me_cell[trix])])\n   574→        except (IndexError, TypeError):\n   575→            continue\n   576→        if me_trial.size == 0:\n   577→            continue\n   578→\n   579→        ft = None\n   580→        if side_cam is not None and trix < len(side_cam['trials']) and side_cam['trials'][trix] is not None:\n   581→            ft = side_cam['trials'][trix]['frameTimes']\n   582→        if ft is None or ft.size == 0 or np.all(np.isnan(ft)):\n   583→            ft = np.arange(1, me_trial.size + 1) / 400.0\n   584→\n   585→        ft_aligned = ft - vidshift - align_times[trix]\n   586→        min_len = min(len(ft_aligned), len(me_trial))\n   587→        ft_aligned, me_trial = ft_aligned[:min_len], me_trial[:min_len]\n   588→        valid = ~np.isnan(ft_aligned) & ~np.isnan(me_trial)\n   589→        if valid.sum() < 2:\n   590→            continue\n   591→        try:\n   592→            me_aligned[:, trix] = interp1d(ft_aligned[valid], me_trial[valid],\n   593→                                           kind='linear', bounds_error=False,\n   594→                                           fill_value=np.nan)(time_axis).astype(np.float32)\n   595→        except Exception:\n   596→            continue\n   597→\n   598→    for trix in range(ntrials):\n   599→        col = me_aligned[:, trix]\n   600→        if not np.isnan(col).all() and np.isnan(col).any():\n   601→            me_aligned[:, trix] = _fill_nearest(col)\n   602→\n   603→    return me_aligned\n   604→\n   605→\n   606→def _compute_feature_velocity_generic(traj_data, ntrials, view, feat_name, vidshift, align_times, time_axis, is_tongue=False):\n   607→    \"\"\"Compute velocity of a DLC feature from traj_data (format-agnostic).\"\"\"\n   608→    n_time = len(time_axis)\n   609→    speed = np.full((n_time, ntrials), np.nan, dtype=np.float32)\n   610→\n   611→    if traj_data is None:\n   612→        return None\n   613→\n   614→    view_idx = view - 1\n   615→    if view_idx >= len(traj_data['views']):\n   616→        return None\n   617→\n   618→    view_data = traj_data['views'][view_idx]\n   619→    feat_names = view_data['feat_names']\n   620→\n   621→    feat_idx = None\n   622→    for idx, fn in enumerate(feat_names):\n   623→        if fn == feat_name:\n   624→            feat_idx = idx\n   625→            break\n   626→    if feat_idx is None:\n   627→        for idx, fn in enumerate(feat_names):\n   628→            if feat_name in fn:\n   629→                feat_idx = idx\n   630→                break\n   631→    if feat_idx is None:\n   632→        return None\n   633→\n   634→    for trix in range(min(ntrials, len(view_data['trials']))):\n   635→        trial_info = view_data['trials'][trix]\n   636→        if trial_info is None:\n   637→            continue\n   638→        try:\n   639→            ts = trial_info['ts']\n   640→            ft = trial_info['frameTimes']\n   641→            ts_fmt = trial_info['ts_format']\n   642→\n   643→            if ts.ndim != 3:\n   644→                continue\n   645→\n   646→            if ts_fmt == 'hdf5':\n   647→                x_raw = ts[feat_idx, 0, :]\n   648→                y_raw = ts[feat_idx, 1, :]\n   649→            else:\n   650→                x_raw = ts[:, 0, feat_idx]\n   651→                y_raw = ts[:, 1, feat_idx]\n   652→\n   653→            if ft.size == 0 or np.all(np.isnan(ft)):\n   654→                ft = np.arange(1, len(x_raw) + 1) / 400.0\n   655→\n   656→            ft_aligned = ft - vidshift - align_times[trix]\n   657→            min_len = min(len(ft_aligned), len(x_raw))\n   658→            ft_a, x_r, y_r = ft_aligned[:min_len], x_raw[:min_len], y_raw[:min_len]\n   659→\n   660→            valid = ~np.isnan(ft_a) & ~np.isnan(x_r) & ~np.isnan(y_r)\n   661→            if valid.sum() < 2:\n   662→                continue\n   663→\n   664→            xpos = interp1d(ft_a[valid], x_r[valid], kind='linear',\n   665→                            bounds_error=False, fill_value=np.nan)(time_axis)\n   666→            ypos = interp1d(ft_a[valid], y_r[valid], kind='linear',\n   667→                            bounds_error=False, fill_value=np.nan)(time_axis)\n   668→\n   669→            if not is_tongue:\n   670→                xpos = _fill_nearest(xpos)\n   671→                ypos = _fill_nearest(ypos)\n   672→\n   673→            xvel = np.gradient(xpos)\n   674→            yvel = np.gradient(ypos)\n   675→\n   676→            if is_tongue:\n   677→                xvel[np.isnan(xvel)] = 0\n   678→                yvel[np.isnan(yvel)] = 0\n   679→            else:\n   680→                base_xvel = np.nanmedian(np.diff(xpos))\n   681→                base_yvel = np.nanmedian(np.diff(ypos))\n   682→                xvel -= (base_xvel if not np.isnan(base_xvel) else 0)\n   683→                yvel -= (base_yvel if not np.isnan(base_yvel) else 0)\n   684→                xvel = _fill_nearest(xvel)\n   685→                yvel = _fill_nearest(yvel)\n   686→\n   687→            speed[:, trix] = np.sqrt(xvel**2 + yvel**2).astype(np.float32)\n   688→        except Exception:\n   689→            continue\n   690→\n   691→    return speed\n   692→\n   693→\n   694→def _fill_nearest(arr):\n   695→    \"\"\"Fill NaN values with nearest non-NaN value.\"\"\"\n   696→    nans = np.isnan(arr)\n   697→    if not nans.any():\n   698→        return arr\n   699→    if nans.all():\n   700→        return arr\n   701→    valid_idx = np.where(~nans)[0]\n   702→    nan_idx = np.where(nans)[0]\n   703→    nearest = np.searchsorted(valid_idx, nan_idx).clip(0, len(valid_idx) - 1)\n   704→    arr[nans] = arr[valid_idx[nearest]]\n   705→    return arr\n   706→\n   707→\n   708→def _discretize_velocity(data, n_timebins, n_trials):\n   709→    \"\"\"Discretize continuous data into 2 bins using 50th percentile per session.\"\"\"\n   710→    if data is None:\n   711→        return np.zeros((n_timebins, n_trials), dtype=np.int64)\n   712→\n   713→    # Compute 50th percentile across all valid (non-NaN) values\n   714→    valid_vals = data[~np.isnan(data)]\n   715→    if valid_vals.size == 0:\n   716→        return np.zeros((n_timebins, n_trials), dtype=np.int64)\n   717→\n   718→    threshold = np.percentile(valid_vals, 50)\n   719→    disc = (data >= threshold).astype(np.int64)\n   720→    # SMOOTHING\n\n#### **mySmooth.m** (utils/)\n**Purpose**: Causal Gaussian smoothing\n**Core logic**:\n```python\ndef causal_gaussian_smooth(x, N, bctype='reflect'):\n    # Build kernel\n    kern = sig_windows.gaussian(N, std=(N-1)/(2*2.5))\n    kern[:N//2] = 0  # CAUSAL: zero out first half\n    kern = kern / kern.sum()  # normalize\n    \n    # Boundary handling\n    if bctype == 'reflect':\n        x_pad = np.concatenate([x[:N, :], x], axis=0)  # reflect first N points\n    elif bctype == 'zeropad':\n        x_pad = np.concatenate([np.zeros((N, x.shape[1])), x], axis=0)\n    \n    # Convolve\n    out = np.convolve(x_pad, kern, mode='same')\n    return out[N:]  # trim padding\n```\n\n**Parameters**:\n- **Window size N**: 15 bins\n- **Standard deviation**: (N-1)/(2*2.5) ≈ 2.8\n- **Causality**: Kernel has zero first half - only looks backward (essential for real-time decoding)\n- **Boundary type**: 'reflect' (default) or 'zeropad'\n- **Applied to**: Binned spike counts before FR conversion\n\n---\n\n### 7. DATA LOADING\n\n#### **loadObjs.m** (DataLoadingScripts/)\n**Purpose**: Load .mat data objects from files\n**Implementation**:\n- Loads MATLAB v7.3 (HDF5) and v5 format files\n- Returns struct `obj` with fields:\n  - `obj.bp`: Trial-level behavioral data (Ntrials, hit, miss, no, early, L, R, autowater, stim.enable, events)\n  - `obj.clu`: Cell array of spike cluster data (1 x nProbes), each with: tm (spike times), trialtm, trial (trial index), quality, site, spkWavs\n  - `obj.traj`: DLC trajectories (2 x 1) for side/bottom cameras - features x 3[x,y,confidence] x frames\n  - `obj.ex`: Session metadata (animal, day, probe.loc, probe.type, probe.depth)\n  - `obj.sglx`: Neural recording params (fs=25kHz, Nchan, padSec=0.5)\n\n#### **loadSessionData.m** (DataLoadingScripts/)\n**Purpose**: Master loading function that processes each session\n**Implementation** (from convert_data.py):\n```python\n# Pipeline: loadObjs -> findTrials -> findClusters -> alignSpikes -> getSeq -> removeLowFRClusters\nFORMAT: auto-detect HDF5 vs v5\nRAW UNITS: typically 100-300 per session\nQUALITY FIELD: quality label per unit (Excellent, Great, Good, Fair, Multi, Poor, garbage, noisy)\n```\n\n---\n\n### 2. TRIAL FILTERING\n\n#### **findTrials.m** (DataLoadingScripts/)\n**Purpose**: Find trial indices matching condition strings\n**Implementation** (from code):\n- Exclude early lick trials: `~early` (early == 0)\n- Exclude photostimulation trials: `~stim.enable` (stim.enable == 0)\n- Filter by context: autowater field (autowater=1 for WC, autowater=0 for DR)\n- Filter by outcome: hit (hit=1 for correct, miss=1 for incorrect)\n- **Default filtering**: Exclude early lick and stimulation trials\n- **Minimum trials**: >= 2 valid trials per session to proceed\n\n**Parameters**:\n```matlab\nquality = 'all'  % matches our quality_exclude={'garbage','noisy','gabrga','real?'}\n```\n\n---\n\n### 3. CLUSTER/NEURON FILTERING\n\n#### **findClusters.m** (DataLoadingScripts/)\n**Purpose**: Filter clusters by quality\n**Implementation**:\n```python\nquality_exclude = {'garbage', 'noisy', 'gabrga', 'real?'}\nkeep_mask = np.array([q not in quality_exclude and q != '' \n                      for q in all_qualities])\n```\n**Key filtering rules**:\n1. Exclude quality labels: 'garbage', 'noisy', 'gabrga', 'real?'\n2. Exclude empty/NaN quality strings\n3. Keep: 'Excellent', 'Great', 'Good', 'Fair', 'Multi', 'Poor'\n\n**Typical impact**: 100-200 raw units → 50-100 after quality filter per session\n\n---\n\n### 4. SPIKE ALIGNMENT AND BINNING\n\n#### **alignSpikes.m** (DataLoadingScripts/)\n**Purpose**: Align spike times to event (goCue)\n**Implementation**:\n```python\nalign_event = 'goCue'\nalign_times = ev['goCue']  # event times in seconds\nspike_time_aligned = spike_time_raw - align_times[trial_num]\n```\n**Key parameters**:\n- **Alignment event**: goCue onset\n- **Baseline**: -2.5s before goCue\n- **Response**: +2.5s after goCue\n- **Total window**: [-2.5, 2.5]s = 5 seconds\n\n#### **getSeq.m** (DataLoadingScripts/)\n**Purpose**: Bin spikes, smooth, compute PSTHs and single-trial data\n**Implementation**:\n```python\n# Binning\ndt = 1.0 / 100  # 10ms bins\nedges = np.arange(tmin, tmax + dt, dt)  # [-2.5, -2.49, ..., 2.49, 2.5]\ncounts, _ = np.histogram(spike_times, bins=edges)  # raw spike counts per bin\n\n# Smoothing (causal Gaussian)\nkern = sig_windows.gaussian(N=15, std=(15-1)/(2*2.5))  # window=15\nkern[:N//2] = 0  # make causal (zero out first half)\nkern = kern / kern.sum()  # normalize\n\n# Fire rate conversion\nfr = convolve(counts / dt, kern)  # spks/sec\n```\n\n**Key parameters**:\n- **Time bin size**: dt = 0.01 s = 10 ms\n- **Bins per trial**: 500 (for 5s window at 10ms)\n- **Smoothing window**: 15 bins\n- **Smoothing type**: Causal Gaussian (only looks backward in time)\n- **Boundary condition**: 'reflect' (pad with reflected signal)\n- **Output shape**: (neurons, 500 timepoints, trials)\n- **Units**: spikes/second (Hz)\n\n---\n\n### 5. LOW FIRING RATE FILTERING\n\n#### **removeLowFRClusters.m** (DataLoadingScripts/)\n**Purpose**: Remove neurons with mean FR < threshold\n**Implementation**:\n```python\nmean_fr = np.mean(trialdat, axis=(1, 2))  # across time and trials\nfr_mask = mean_fr > params['low_fr']\ntrialdat = trialdat[fr_mask, :, :]\n```\n\n**Key parameters**:\n- **Low FR threshold**: 1.0 Hz (mean across all timepoints and trials)\n- **Typical impact**: Remove ~10-20% of neurons per session\n- **Min session units**: Sessions with < 10 units after filtering are excluded\n- **Sessions passing filter**: All 44 sessions have >= 17 units\n\n---\n\n### 6. SMOOTHING\n\n#### **mySmooth.m** (utils/)\n**Purpose**: Causal Gaussian smoothing\n**Core logic**:\n```python\ndef causal_gaussian_smooth(x, N, bctype='reflect'):\n    # Build kernel\n    kern = sig_windows.gaussian(N, std=(N-1)/(2*2.5))\n    kern[:N//2] = 0  # CAUSAL: zero out first half\n    kern = kern / kern.sum()  # normalize\n    \n    # Boundary handling\n    if bctype == 'reflect':\n        x_pad = np.concatenate([x[:N, :], x], axis=0)  # reflect first N points\n    elif bctype == 'zeropad':\n        x_pad = np.concatenate([np.zeros((N, x.shape[1])), x], axis=0)\n    \n    # Convolve\n    out = np.convolve(x_pad, kern, mode='same')\n    return out[N:]  # trim padding\n```\n\n**Parameters**:\n- **Window size N**: 15 bins\n- **Standard deviation**: (N-1)/(2*2.5) ≈ 2.8\n- **Causality**: Kernel has zero first half - only looks backward (essential for real-time decoding)\n- **Boundary type**: 'reflect' (default) or 'zeropad'\n- **Applied to**: Binned spike counts before FR conversion\n\n---\n\n### 7. KINEMATICS COMPUTATION\n\n#### **getKinematicsFromVideo.m** (funcs/kinematics/)\n**Purpose**: Extract kinematics from DLC trajectories\n\n#### **findPosition.m** (funcs/kinematics/)\n**Purpose**: Extract position from DLC with video offset\n**Implementation**:\n```python\n# DLC trajectory format handling\nif format == 'hdf5':\n    x_raw = ts[feat_idx, 0, :]      # (nFeats, 3[x,y,conf], nFrames)\n    y_raw = ts[feat_idx, 1, :]\nelse:  # v5\n    x_raw = ts[:, 0, feat_idx]      # (nFrames, 3[x,y,conf], nFeats)\n    y_raw = ts[:, 1, feat_idx]\n\n# Video offset correction\nft_aligned = ft - vidshift - align_times[trix]\n\n# Interpolate to neural time axis\nxpos = interp1d(ft[valid], x_raw[valid], kind='linear')(time_axis)\n```\n\n#### **findVelocity.m** (funcs/kinematics/)\n**Purpose**: Compute velocity from position\n**Implementation**:\n```python\n# Velocity: gradient of position\nxvel = np.gradient(xpos)\nyvel = np.gradient(ypos)\n\n# Euclidean speed\nspeed = np.sqrt(xvel**2 + yvel**2)\n```\n\n**Features extracted** (from DLC):\n- **Tongue (bottom camera)**: 'top_tongue' feature\n- **Paw (bottom camera)**: 'top_paw' feature\n- **Method**: Velocity magnitude (Euclidean: sqrt(vx² + vy²))\n- **Time alignment**: Interpolate to 10ms neural time axis\n\n---\n\n### 8. MOTION ENERGY LOADING\n\n#### **loadMotionEnergy.m** (DataLoadingScripts/)\n**Purpose**: Load and align motion energy data\n**Implementation**:\n```python\n# Load motion energy\nme_file = sio.loadmat(me_fn)  or h5py.File(me_fn)\nme_struct = me_file['me']\nme_cell = [per_trial_array for each trial]  # 400 Hz\n\n# Align to neural time axis\nft_aligned = ft - vidshift - align_times[trix]\nme_aligned = interp1d(ft[valid], me[valid], kind='linear')(time_axis)\n```\n\n**Parameters**:\n- **Source**: Separate `motionEnergy_<animal>_<date>.mat` files\n- **Sampling rate**: 400 Hz (original)\n- **Resampling**: Interpolated to neural time axis (10ms bins)\n- **Video offset**: Subtract vidshift (computed from bitcode sync)\n- **Missing data**: Some sessions (7/44) have corrupted or missing ME files (filled with zeros)\n\n---\n\n### 9. VIDEO OFFSET COMPUTATION\n\n#### **findVideoOffset.m** (utils/)\n**Purpose**: Compute temporal offset between neural and video\n**Implementation** (from code):\n```python\nvidshift = 0.5  # default\ntry:\n    bit_start = np.nanmedian(bp_group['ev']['bitStart'][()])\n    fs = sglx['fs'][()]  # 25 kHz\n    bitcode_bitstart = np.nanmedian(sglx['bitcode']['bitstart'][()])\n    vid_file_offset = bitcode_bitstart / fs\n    vidshift = vid_file_offset - bit_start\nexcept:\n    vidshift = 0.5\n```\n\n**Parameters**:\n- **Default vidshift**: 0.5 s\n- **Method**: Uses bitcode synchronization between SpikeGLX and camera\n- **Application**: Subtract from all video timestamps before alignment to neural events\n\n---\n\n### 10. TUTORIAL/EXAMPLE\n\n#### **WorkingWithDataObjs.m**\n**Purpose**: MATLAB guide for working with data objects\n**Key parameters documented** (CONVERSION_NOTES Step 1):\n```matlab\nparams.alignEvent = 'goCue'\nparams.lowFR = 1 Hz\nparams.tmin = -2.5\nparams.tmax = 2.5\nparams.dt = 1/100  % 10ms bins\nparams.smooth = 15\nparams.quality = 'all'  % excludes garbage/noisy/gabrga/real?\n```\n\n**Output format**:\n- `obj.trialdat`: (time, neurons, trials) shaped array\n- Units: spikes/second (Hz)\n- Time axis: -2.5 to 2.5 s at 10ms resolution = 500 timepoints\n\n---\n\n### 11. ↓\n[Extract behavioral outputs]\n    ├─ lick_direction: from bp.R (0=L, 1=R)\n    ├─ behavioral_context: from bp.autowater (0=WC, 1=DR)\n    └─ outcome: from bp.hit (0=incorrect, 1=correct)\n axis]\n    ↓\n[Discretize continuous outputs]\n    ├─ 50th percentile threshold per session\n    ├─ tongue_velocity → binary [low, high]\n    ├─ paw_velocity → binary [low, high]\n    └─ motion_energy → binary [low, high]\n```\n\n---\n\n## KEY PARAMETERS AND DEFAULTS\n\n| Parameter | Value | Reference |\n|-----------|-------|-----------|\n| Align event | goCue | WorkingWithDataObjs.m |\n| Time In this paradigm, head-fixed mice performed two directional licking tasks that alternated block-wise within each behavioral session. These tasks varied in their cognitive demands but required the same instructed motor output.\n     4→\n     5→This paradigm included an established delayed-response (DR) task in which motor planning is temporally dissociated from movement execution (Fig. 1a). In the DR task, an auditory stimulus indicated the location of a reward. After a brief delay epoch with no auditory stimuli, a separate auditory ‘go cue’ instructed the mouse to move. If the mouse performed a directional tongue movement to the correct target, a water reward was delivered. In separate blocks of trials, mice performed a water-cued (WC) task in which all auditory stimuli were omitted. Instead, a drop of water was presented at a random point in time at a randomly selected reward port. Animals consumed water upon its presentation. Mice received no explicit cues signaling DR and WC blocks. The DR task required a perceptual decision that guides the planning of a subsequent motor action. In the WC task, uncertainty in the timing and location of reward prevented formation of a motor plan before reward presentation, and, instead, mice detected reward availability using olfactory and/or vibrissal cues. This structure required animals to maintain an internal representation of the current block identity (context) to maintain high task performance.\n     6→\n...\n</persisted-output>","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_018GZkURApAxoyToQMcPavNZ","session_id":"ebe28397-bb10-4c31-a32c-9ad7ad75d539","uuid":"7524f6f1-f92a-4146-a436-f8a181cedc4d","timestamp":"2026-08-03T14:50:54.262Z"}
